# Brand sources

Where each carrier's colour and decal come from. Catalog colours come from Universal
Parcel Scraper; app palettes and decals live in [`identities.json`](identities.json)
and [`truck.json`](truck.json).

- Colours are small UI accents, not print specifications. They come from the carrier's logo SVG or
  website palette unless marked approximate.
- No logo files are bundled, fetched or committed. Decals are drawn locally from polygons, segments
  and circles: simplified marks, and names set in the app's own single-stroke letters, not in the
  carrier's lettering.
- Carriers missing from these tables have no recorded source.

| `zajel` | [zajel.com](https://zajel.com/icon.png) — site icon PNG single fill #36b936, a dove drawn in one outline; the header logo, an inline SVG, sets the same dove in #36b936 between zajel and زاجل in dark green #132818 |
| `zajil` | [zajil-express.com](https://zajil-express.com/_next/static/media/logo.3fe78f13.svg) — logo SVG fill #ffc20e, theme-color #ffc20a; dark #02232a from the site's overlays |

| `zeleris` | [zeleris.com](https://www.zeleris.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/LOGO-HORIZONTAL.svg) — logo SVG, its only fill is #9be51b |

| `zheldor` | [jde.ru](https://jde.ru/) — mask-icon colour #e6332a; the logo SVG fill is #e6342a |

| `zippd` | [zippd.com](https://www.zippd.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/zippd-logo-blue.png) — logo PNG flat blue, median #173ded of its 149,322 coloured pixels, its only colour |
| `zippee` | [zippee.delivery](https://zippee-delivery-assets.s3.ap-south-1.amazonaws.com/crispy-zippee/assets/logos/zippee_dark.png) — header logo PNG single near-black #201e1e for a gabled frame holding an L of seven small squares, beside the Zippee wordmark and ®; the [site icon](https://www.zippee.delivery/icon.png) is the frame alone in near-black #1b1a17; the catalog colour #9f3da8 is the site CSS's primary colour, rgb(159 61 168), for its buttons, links and highlights |
| `znlogic` | [tracking.link](https://tracking.link/static/img/zinc-tracker-logo.png) — tracker logo PNG, a delivery truck drawn as a cardboard box: tan #ac7138 for the box's side, its most used colour, the lit faces lighter, about #e5ab66, a pale tape strip, about #f8dbad, over the top, and a dark brown Z with this-side-up arrows, about #693c18, on the side |
